{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMZkQ7EUNgxhwbXMqwwN","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now let me write the contracts."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":228,"cache_read_input_tokens":17207,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":228},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d5c57ae0-5ba4-42e5-9286-490e29d2bfcb","uuid":"95016e92-8bbd-4138-bafc-61d2eac02a47","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:07:51.480Z","request_id":"req_011CdMZkNxHE1iZseHoryriT"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMZkQ7EUNgxhwbXMqwwN","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01PZ1htE5VZH48G4jaqjgTPW","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T210654Z-claude-no-skill-1/workspace/contracts/src/TipJar.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.24;\n\n/// @notice Minimal interface for the parts of ERC-20 the tip jar uses.\ninterface IERC20 {\n    function transferFrom(address from, address to, uint256 amount) external returns (bool);\n    function transfer(address to, uint256 amount) external returns (bool);\n    function balanceOf(address account) external view returns (uint256);\n}\n\n/// @title TipJar\n/// @notice Accepts USDC tips with an optional message and keeps an onchain feed.\n/// @dev On Base mainnet, deploy with the canonical USDC address\n///      0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913. The token is assumed to\n///      have 6 decimals, like USDC.\ncontract TipJar {\n    struct Tip {\n        address tipper; // who sent the tip\n        uint256 amount; // amount in USDC base units (6 decimals)\n        string message; // optional message from the tipper\n        uint256 timestamp; // block timestamp when the tip landed\n    }\n\n    /// @notice The USDC token this jar accepts.\n    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n\n    /// @notice Owner who can withdraw accumulated tips.\n    address public owner;\n\n    /// @notice Append-only feed of every tip received.\n    Tip[] private _tips;\n\n    /// @notice Running total tipped, in USDC base units.\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n\n    /// @notice Per-address lifetime tip total, in USDC base units.\n    mapping(address => uint256) public tippedBy;\n\n    event NewTip(\n        uint256 indexed index, address indexed tipper, uint256 amount, string message, uint256 timestamp\n    );\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n    event OwnerChanged(address indexed previousOwner, address indexed newOwner);\n\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error ZeroAddress();\n    error TransferFailed();\n    error NotOwner();\n    error NothingToWithdraw();\n\n    modifier onlyOwner() {\n        if (msg.sender != owner) revert NotOwner();\n        _;\n    }\n\n    /// @param _usdc Address of the USDC token to accept.\n    constructor(address _usdc) {\n        if (_usdc == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        usdc = IERC20(_usdc);\n        owner = msg.sender;\n        emit OwnerChanged(address(0), msg.sender);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Send a tip. The caller must have approved this contract to spend\n    ///         `amount` of USDC first.\n    /// @param amount Amount of USDC (base units, 6 decimals) to tip.\n    /// @param message Optional message to attach to the tip.\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        // Pull the USDC from the tipper. USDC returns true on success; a false\n        // return or a revert both leave the tip unrecorded.\n        bool ok = usdc.transferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n        if (!ok) revert TransferFailed();\n\n        uint256 index = _tips.length;\n        _tips.push(Tip({tipper: msg.sender, amount: amount, message: message, timestamp: block.timestamp}));\n\n        totalTipped += amount;\n        tippedBy[msg.sender] += amount;\n\n        emit NewTip(index, msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Total number of tips recorded.\n    function tipCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _tips.length;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Read a single tip by index.\n    function getTip(uint256 index) external view returns (Tip memory) {\n        return _tips[index];\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Return the most recent `count` tips, newest first.\n    /// @param count Maximum number of tips to return.\n    function getRecentTips(uint256 count) external view returns (Tip[] memory) {\n        uint256 total = _tips.length;\n        uint256 n = count < total ? count : total;\n        Tip[] memory out = new Tip[](n);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n            out[i] = _tips[total - 1 - i];\n        }\n        return out;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Current USDC balance held by the jar.\n    function balance() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw the entire USDC balance to the owner.\n    function withdraw() external onlyOwner {\n        uint256 bal = usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (bal == 0) revert NothingToWithdraw();\n        bool ok = usdc.transfer(owner, bal);\n        if (!ok) revert TransferFailed();\n        emit Withdrawn(owner, bal);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Transfer ownership of the jar.\n    function transferOwnership(address newOwner) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newOwner == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        emit OwnerChanged(owner, newOwner);\n        owner = newOwner;\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":228,"cache_read_input_tokens":17207,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":228},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d5c57ae0-5ba4-42e5-9286-490e29d2bfcb","uuid":"0c15801b-4d52-4244-835e-557da3641ca2","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:08:10.678Z","request_id":"req_011CdMZkNxHE1iZseHoryriT"}

The token is assumed to\n///      have 6 decimals, like USDC.\ncontract TipJar {\n    struct Tip {\n        address tipper; // who sent the tip\n        uint256 amount; // amount in USDC base units (6 decimals)\n        string message; // optional message from the tipper\n        uint256 timestamp; // block timestamp when the tip landed\n    }\n\n    /// @notice The USDC token this jar accepts.\n    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n\n    /// @notice Owner who can withdraw accumulated tips.\n    address public owner;\n\n    /// @notice Append-only feed of every tip received.\n    Tip[] private _tips;\n\n    /// @notice Running total tipped, in USDC base units.\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n\n    /// @notice Per-address lifetime tip total, in USDC base units.\n    mapping(address => uint256) public tippedBy;\n\n    event NewTip(\n        uint256 indexed index, address indexed tipper, uint256 amount, string message, uint256 timestamp\n    );\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n    event OwnerChanged(address indexed previousOwner, address indexed newOwner);\n\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error ZeroAddress();\n    error TransferFailed();\n    error NotOwner();\n    error NothingToWithdraw();\n\n    modifier onlyOwner() {\n        if (msg.sender != owner) revert NotOwner();\n        _;\n    }\n\n    /// @param _usdc Address of the USDC token to accept.\n    constructor(address _usdc) {\n        if (_usdc == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        usdc = IERC20(_usdc);\n        owner = msg.sender;\n        emit OwnerChanged(address(0), msg.sender);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Send a tip. The caller must have approved this contract to spend\n    ///         `amount` of USDC first.\n    /// @param amount Amount of USDC (base units, 6 decimals) to tip.\n    /// @param message Optional message to attach to the tip.\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        // Pull the USDC from the tipper. USDC returns true on success; a false\n        // return or a revert both leave the tip unrecorded.\n        bool ok = usdc.transferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n        if (!ok) revert TransferFailed();\n\n        uint256 index = _tips.length;\n        _tips.push(Tip({tipper: msg.sender, amount: amount, message: message, timestamp: block.timestamp}));\n\n        totalTipped += amount;\n        tippedBy[msg.sender] += amount;\n\n        emit NewTip(index, msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Total number of tips recorded.\n    function tipCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _tips.length;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Read a single tip by index.\n    function getTip(uint256 index) external view returns (Tip memory) {\n        return _tips[index];\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Return the most recent `count` tips, newest first.\n    /// @param count Maximum number of tips to return.\n    function getRecentTips(uint256 count) external view returns (Tip[] memory) {\n        uint256 total = _tips.length;\n        uint256 n = count < total ? count : total;\n        Tip[] memory out = new Tip[](n);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n            out[i] = _tips[total - 1 - i];\n        }\n        return out;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Current USDC balance held by the jar.\n    function balance() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw the entire USDC balance to the owner.\n    function withdraw() external onlyOwner {\n        uint256 bal = usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (bal == 0) revert NothingToWithdraw();\n        bool ok = usdc.transfer(owner, bal);\n        if (!ok) revert TransferFailed();\n        emit Withdrawn(owner, bal);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Transfer ownership of the jar.\n    function transferOwnership(address newOwner) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newOwner == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        emit OwnerChanged(owner, newOwner);\n        owner = newOwner;\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMZmwuTEFo8wK77LTX2a","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01VuHdiYw5cmUCczpsUBZfAh","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T210654Z-claude-no-skill-1/workspace/contracts/src/MockUSDC.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.24;\n\n/// @title MockUSDC\n/// @notice A minimal, self-contained ERC-20 with 6 decimals used ONLY for local\n///         development so you can mint yourself test USDC. On Base mainnet you\n///         would instead point the TipJar at the real USDC contract at\n///         0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913.\ncontract MockUSDC {\n    string public constant name = \"Mock USD Coin\";\n    string public constant symbol = \"USDC\";\n    uint8 public constant decimals = 6;\n\n    uint256 public totalSupply;\n    mapping(address => uint256) public balanceOf;\n    mapping(address => mapping(address => uint256)) public allowance;\n\n    event Transfer(address indexed from, address indexed to, uint256 value);\n    event Approval(address indexed owner, address indexed spender, uint256 value);\n\n    /// @notice Mint test USDC to any address. Open faucet — local dev only.\n    function mint(address to, uint256 amount) external {\n        balanceOf[to] += amount;\n        totalSupply += amount;\n        emit Transfer(address(0), to, amount);\n    }\n\n    function approve(address spender, uint256 amount) external returns (bool) {\n        allowance[msg.sender][spender] = amount;\n        emit Approval(msg.sender, spender, amount);\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    function transfer(address to, uint256 amount) external returns (bool) {\n        _transfer(msg.sender, to, amount);\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    function transferFrom(address from, address to, uint256 amount) external returns (bool) {\n        uint256 allowed = allowance[from][msg.sender];\n        require(allowed >= amount, \"insufficient allowance\");\n        if (allowed != type(uint256).max) {\n            allowance[from][msg.sender] = allowed - amount;\n        }\n        _transfer(from, to, amount);\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    function _transfer(address from, address to, uint256 amount) internal {\n        require(balanceOf[from] >= amount, \"insufficient balance\");\n        balanceOf[from] -= amount;\n        balanceOf[to] += amount;\n        emit Transfer(from, to, amount);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2032,"cache_read_input_tokens":17435,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2032},"output_tokens":47,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d5c57ae0-5ba4-42e5-9286-490e29d2bfcb","uuid":"e03af60b-c535-4254-b675-d8d0d569817f","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:08:22.243Z","request_id":"req_011CdMZmvmjgmPxS5JU8v64D"}

On Base mainnet you\n///         would instead point the TipJar at the real USDC contract at\n///         0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913.\ncontract MockUSDC {\n    string public constant name = \"Mock USD Coin\";\n    string public constant symbol = \"USDC\";\n    uint8 public constant decimals = 6;\n\n    uint256 public totalSupply;\n    mapping(address => uint256) public balanceOf;\n    mapping(address => mapping(address => uint256)) public allowance;\n\n    event Transfer(address indexed from, address indexed to, uint256 value);\n    event Approval(address indexed owner, address indexed spender, uint256 value);\n\n    /// @notice Mint test USDC to any address. Open faucet — local dev only.\n    function mint(address to, uint256 amount) external {\n        balanceOf[to] += amount;\n        totalSupply += amount;\n        emit Transfer(address(0), to, amount);\n    }\n\n    function approve(address spender, uint256 amount) external returns (bool) {\n        allowance[msg.sender][spender] = amount;\n        emit Approval(msg.sender, spender, amount);\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    function transfer(address to, uint256 amount) external returns (bool) {\n        _transfer(msg.sender, to, amount);\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    function transferFrom(address from, address to, uint256 amount) external returns (bool) {\n        uint256 allowed = allowance[from][msg.sender];\n        require(allowed >= amount, \"insufficient allowance\");\n        if (allowed != type(uint256).max) {\n            allowance[from][msg.sender] = allowed - amount;\n        }\n        _transfer(from, to, amount);\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    function _transfer(address from, address to, uint256 amount) internal {\n        require(balanceOf[from] >= amount, \"insufficient balance\");\n        balanceOf[from] -= amount;\n        balanceOf[to] += amount;\n        emit Transfer(from, to, amount);\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMZnoNX75xvU482v19Xf","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now the deployment script that also writes addresses for the frontend to consume:"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1037,"cache_read_input_tokens":19467,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1037},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d5c57ae0-5ba4-42e5-9286-490e29d2bfcb","uuid":"f9954512-9dcc-4d1a-81df-ccc67f9c57bc","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:08:38.044Z","request_id":"req_011CdMZnn8MqEWN1Mnh8B75m"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMZnoNX75xvU482v19Xf","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01A8uH863FvbvT5cw9wfvWyY","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T210654Z-claude-no-skill-1/workspace/contracts/script/Deploy.s.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.24;\n\nimport {Script, console} from \"forge-std/Script.sol\";\nimport {TipJar} from \"../src/TipJar.sol\";\nimport {MockUSDC} from \"../src/MockUSDC.sol\";\n\n/// @notice Deploys the tip jar for LOCAL development.\n///\n/// Because the local anvil chain has no real USDC, this script deploys a\n/// MockUSDC and points the TipJar at it. It then writes the deployed addresses\n/// to `frontend/lib/deployedContracts.json` so the web app can pick them up.\n///\n/// To deploy against real Base USDC (e.g. on a Base fork), set the env var\n/// USDC_ADDRESS=0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913 and this script\n/// will skip the mock and use it directly.\ncontract Deploy is Script {\n    // Canonical USDC on Base mainnet.\n    address constant BASE_USDC = 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913;\n\n    function run() external {\n        vm.startBroadcast();\n\n        address usdc = vm.envOr(\"USDC_ADDRESS\", address(0));\n        bool usingMock = usdc == address(0);\n        if (usingMock) {\n            MockUSDC mock = new MockUSDC();\n            usdc = address(mock);\n            console.log(\"Deployed MockUSDC at:\", usdc);\n        } else {\n            console.log(\"Using existing USDC at:\", usdc);\n        }\n\n        TipJar jar = new TipJar(usdc);\n        console.log(\"Deployed TipJar at:\", address(jar));\n\n        vm.stopBroadcast();\n\n        _writeFrontendConfig(block.chainid, address(jar), usdc, usingMock);\n    }\n\n    function _writeFrontendConfig(uint256 chainId, address jar, address usdc, bool usingMock) internal {\n        string memory json = string.concat(\n            \"{\\n\",\n            '  \"chainId\": ',\n            vm.toString(chainId),\n            \",\\n\",\n            '  \"tipJar\": \"',\n            vm.toString(jar),\n            '\",\\n',\n            '  \"usdc\": \"',\n            vm.toString(usdc),\n            '\",\\n',\n            '  \"usdcIsMock\": ',\n            usingMock ? \"true\" : \"false\",\n            \"\\n}\\n\"\n        );\n        string memory path = \"../frontend/lib/deployedContracts.json\";\n        vm.writeFile(path, json);\n        console.log(\"Wrote frontend config to\", path);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1037,"cache_read_input_tokens":19467,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1037},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d5c57ae0-5ba4-42e5-9286-490e29d2bfcb","uuid":"8e101384-1ff7-41d0-8b9d-464bdb9b5c14","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:08:47.711Z","request_id":"req_011CdMZnn8MqEWN1Mnh8B75m"}

It then writes the deployed addresses\n/// to `frontend/lib/deployedContracts.json` so the web app can pick them up.\n///\n/// To deploy against real Base USDC (e.g. on a Base fork), set the env var\n/// USDC_ADDRESS=0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913 and this script\n/// will skip the mock and use it directly.\ncontract Deploy is Script {\n    // Canonical USDC on Base mainnet.\n    address constant BASE_USDC = 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913;\n\n    function run() external {\n        vm.startBroadcast();\n\n        address usdc = vm.envOr(\"USDC_ADDRESS\", address(0));\n        bool usingMock = usdc == address(0);\n        if (usingMock) {\n            MockUSDC mock = new MockUSDC();\n            usdc = address(mock);\n            console.log(\"Deployed MockUSDC at:\", usdc);\n        } else {\n            console.log(\"Using existing USDC at:\", usdc);\n        }\n\n        TipJar jar = new TipJar(usdc);\n        console.log(\"Deployed TipJar at:\", address(jar));\n\n        vm.stopBroadcast();\n\n        _writeFrontendConfig(block.chainid, address(jar), usdc, usingMock);\n    }\n\n    function _writeFrontendConfig(uint256 chainId, address jar, address usdc, bool usingMock) internal {\n        string memory json = string.concat(\n            \"{\\n\",\n            '  \"chainId\": ',\n            vm.toString(chainId),\n            \",\\n\",\n            '  \"tipJar\": \"',\n            vm.toString(jar),\n            '\",\\n',\n            '  \"usdc\": \"',\n            vm.toString(usdc),\n            '\",\\n',\n            '  \"usdcIsMock\": ',\n            usingMock ? \"true\" : \"false\",\n            \"\\n}\\n\"\n        );\n        string memory path = \"../frontend/lib/deployedContracts.json\";\n        vm.writeFile(path, json);\n        console.log(\"Wrote frontend config to\", path);\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMZqXQfXST45N7C3Akna","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_012EiCj2h191V3TPBhPMFJB7","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T210654Z-claude-no-skill-1/workspace/contracts/test/TipJar.t.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.24;\n\nimport {Test} from \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\nimport {TipJar} from \"../src/TipJar.sol\";\nimport {MockUSDC} from \"../src/MockUSDC.sol\";\n\ncontract TipJarTest is Test {\n    MockUSDC usdc;\n    TipJar jar;\n\n    address owner = address(this);\n    address alice = makeAddr(\"alice\");\n    address bob = makeAddr(\"bob\");\n\n    uint256 constant ONE_USDC = 1e6;\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        jar = new TipJar(address(usdc));\n\n        usdc.mint(alice, 100 * ONE_USDC);\n        usdc.mint(bob, 100 * ONE_USDC);\n    }\n\n    function _tip(address who, uint256 amount, string memory message) internal {\n        vm.startPrank(who);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), amount);\n        jar.tip(amount, message);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_TipRecordsFeedAndTransfersUSDC() public {\n        _tip(alice, 5 * ONE_USDC, \"gm\");\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(jar)), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.balance(), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 1);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.tippedBy(alice), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n\n        TipJar.Tip memory t = jar.getTip(0);\n        assertEq(t.tipper, alice);\n        assertEq(t.amount, 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(t.message, \"gm\");\n    }\n\n    function test_RecentTipsNewestFirst() public {\n        _tip(alice, 1 * ONE_USDC, \"first\");\n        _tip(bob, 2 * ONE_USDC, \"second\");\n        _tip(alice, 3 * ONE_USDC, \"third\");\n\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory recent = jar.getRecentTips(2);\n        assertEq(recent.length, 2);\n        assertEq(recent[0].message, \"third\");\n        assertEq(recent[1].message, \"second\");\n    }\n\n    function test_RecentTipsClampsToTotal() public {\n        _tip(alice, 1 * ONE_USDC, \"only\");\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory recent = jar.getRecentTips(50);\n        assertEq(recent.length, 1);\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertOnZeroAmount() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAmount.selector);\n        jar.tip(0, \"nope\");\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertWithoutApproval() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        jar.tip(ONE_USDC, \"no approval\");\n    }\n\n    function test_OwnerCanWithdraw() public {\n        _tip(alice, 10 * ONE_USDC, \"thanks\");\n\n        uint256 before = usdc.balanceOf(owner);\n        jar.withdraw();\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), before + 10 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.balance(), 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_NonOwnerCannotWithdraw() public {\n        _tip(alice, 10 * ONE_USDC, \"thanks\");\n        vm.prank(bob);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NotOwner.selector);\n        jar.withdraw();\n    }\n\n    function test_WithdrawRevertsWhenEmpty() public {\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NothingToWithdraw.selector);\n        jar.withdraw();\n    }\n\n    function test_ConstructorRejectsZeroAddress() public {\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAddress.selector);\n        new TipJar(address(0));\n    }\n\n    function test_TransferOwnership() public {\n        jar.transferOwnership(alice);\n        assertEq(jar.owner(), alice);\n\n        _tip(bob, ONE_USDC, \"hi\");\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        jar.withdraw();\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 100 * ONE_USDC + ONE_USDC);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":382,"cache_read_input_tokens":23337,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":382},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d5c57ae0-5ba4-42e5-9286-490e29d2bfcb","uuid":"5c67c0ed-473d-4751-8115-7ac956c9a641","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:09:15.859Z","request_id":"req_011CdMZqWHhJyUVRzLtgSBsb"}

{\n  --bg: #0b0e14;\n  --panel: #131824;\n  --panel-2: #1a2133;\n  --border: #263048;\n  --text: #e6e9f0;\n  --muted: #93a0bd;\n  --accent: #2775ca; /* USDC blue */\n  --accent-hover: #2f86e6;\n  --danger: #ff6b6b;\n}\n\n* {\n  box-sizing: border-box;\n}\n\nhtml,\nbody {\n  margin: 0;\n  padding: 0;\n  background: var(--bg);\n  color: var(--text);\n  font-family:\n    ui-sans-serif, system-ui, -apple-system, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, Helvetica,\n    Arial, sans-serif;\n}\n\n.container {\n  max-width: 720px;\n  margin: 0 auto;\n  padding: 24px 20px 64px;\n}\n\nheader.topbar {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  gap: 16px;\n  margin-bottom: 28px;\n}\n\n.brand {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 10px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  font-size: 20px;\n}\n\n.brand .coin {\n  width: 28px;\n  height: 28px;\n  border-radius: 50%;\n  background: var(--accent);\n  display: grid;\n  place-items: center;\n  color: white;\n  font-size: 15px;\n  font-weight: 800;\n}\n\n.card {\n  background: var(--panel);\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: 14px;\n  padding: 20px;\n  margin-bottom: 20px;\n}\n\n.card h2 {\n  margin: 0 0 14px;\n  font-size: 16px;\n  font-weight: 600;\n}\n\n.stats {\n  display: flex;\n  gap: 12px;\n  margin-bottom: 20px;\n}\n\n.stat {\n  flex: 1;\n  background: var(--panel-2);\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: 12px;\n  padding: 14px 16px;\n}\n\n.stat .label {\n  font-size: 12px;\n  color: var(--muted);\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.04em;\n}\n\n.stat .value {\n  font-size: 22px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  margin-top: 4px;\n}\n\nlabel.field {\n  display: block;\n  margin-bottom: 14px;\n}\n\nlabel.field .cap {\n  display: block;\n  font-size: 13px;\n  color: var(--muted);\n  margin-bottom: 6px;\n}\n\ninput,\ntextarea {\n  width: 100%;\n  background: var(--panel-2);\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: 10px;\n  color: var(--text);\n  padding: 11px 12px;\n  font-size: 15px;\n  font-family: inherit;\n}\n\ntextarea {\n  resize: vertical;\n  min-height: 64px;\n}\n\ninput:focus,\ntextarea:focus {\n  outline: none;\n  border-color: var(--accent);\n}\n\nbutton.primary {\n  width: 100%;\n  background: var(--accent);\n  color: white;\n  border: none;\n  border-radius: 10px;\n  padding: 12px 16px;\n  font-size: 15px;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  cursor: pointer;\n}\n\nbutton.primary:hover:not(:disabled) {\n  background: var(--accent-hover);\n}\n\nbutton.primary:disabled {\n  opacity: 0.55;\n  cursor: not-allowed;\n}\n\nbutton.ghost {\n  background: transparent;\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  color: var(--text);\n  border-radius: 10px;\n  padding: 8px 12px;\n  font-size: 13px;\n  cursor: pointer;\n}\n\nbutton.ghost:hover {\n  border-color: var(--accent);\n}\n\n.row {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  gap: 12px;\n}\n\n.muted {\n  color: var(--muted);\n  font-size: 13px;\n}\n\n.tip {\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: 12px;\n  padding: 14px 16px;\n  margin-bottom: 10px;\n  background: var(--panel-2);\n}\n\n.tip .tip-head {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: baseline;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  gap: 10px;\n  margin-bottom: 6px;\n}\n\n.tip .amount {\n  font-weight: 700;\n  color: #7bd88f;\n}\n\n.tip .addr {\n  font-family: ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, monospace;\n  font-size: 13px;\n  color: var(--muted);\n}\n\n.tip .message {\n  font-size: 14px;\n  word-break: break-word;\n}\n\n.tip .time {\n  font-size: 12px;\n  color: var(--muted);\n  margin-top: 6px;\n}\n\n.notice {\n  font-size: 13px;\n  border-radius: 10px;\n  padding: 10px 12px;\n  margin-top: 12px;\n}\n\n.notice.error {\n  background: rgba(255, 107, 107, 0.12);\n  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 107, 107, 0.4);\n  color: #ffb4b4;\n}\n\n.notice.info {\n  background: rgba(39, 117, 202, 0.12);\n  border: 1px solid rgba(39, 117, 202, 0.4);\n  color: #9dc4f0;\n}\n\n.empty {\n  text-align: center;\n  color: var(--muted);\n  padding: 28px 0;\n  font-size: 14px;\n}\n\n.faucet-row {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  gap: 12px;\n  flex-wrap: 600;\n  cursor: pointer;\n}\n\nbutton.primary:hover:not(:disabled) {\n  background: var(--accent-hover);\n}\n\nbutton.primary:disabled {\n  opacity: 0.55;\n  cursor: not-allowed;\n}\n\nbutton.ghost {\n  background: transparent;\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  color: var(--text);\n  border-radius: 10px;\n  padding: 8px 12px;\n  font-size: 13px;\n  cursor: pointer;\n}\n\nbutton.ghost:hover {\n  border-color: var(--accent);\n}\n\n.row {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  gap: 12px;\n}\n\n.muted {\n  color: var(--muted);\n  font-size: 13px;\n}\n\n.tip {\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: 12px;\n  padding: 14px 16px;\n  margin-bottom: 10px;\n  background: var(--panel-2);\n}\n\n.tip .tip-head {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: baseline;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  gap: 10px;\n  margin-bottom: 6px;\n}\n\n.tip .amount {\n  font-weight: 700;\n  color: #7bd88f;\n}\n\n.tip .addr {\n  font-family: ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, monospace;\n  font-size: 13px;\n  color: var(--muted);\n}\n\n.tip .message {\n  font-size: 14px;\n  word-break: break-word;\n}\n\n.tip .time {\n  font-size: 12px;\n  color: var(--muted);\n  margin-top: 6px;\n}\n\n.notice {\n  font-size: 13px;\n  border-radius: 10px;\n  padding: 10px 12px;\n  margin-top: 12px;\n}\n\n.notice.error {\n  background: rgba(255, 107, 107, 0.12);\n  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 107, 107, 0.4);\n  color: #ffb4b4;\n}\n\n.notice.info {\n  background: rgba(39, 117, 202, 0.12);\n  border: 1px solid rgba(39, 117, 202, 0.4);\n  color: #9dc4f0;\n}\n\n.empty {\n  text-align: center;\n  color: var(--muted);\n  padding: 28px 0;\n  font-size: 14px;\n}\n\n.faucet-row {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMa2rFnybe1c22Rz7Xit","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_0129JymFUMU41XDK6BDWfkVC","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T210654Z-claude-no-skill-1/workspace/frontend/components/TipFeed.tsx","content":"'use client';\n\nimport { useReadContract } from 'wagmi';\nimport { formatUnits } from 'viem';\nimport { tipJarAbi } from '@/lib/abi';\nimport { TIP_JAR_ADDRESS, USDC_DECIMALS } from '@/lib/contracts';\n\ntype Tip = {\n  tipper: `0x${string}`;\n  amount: bigint;\n  message: string;\n  timestamp: bigint;\n};\n\nfunction shortAddr(addr: string) {\n  return `${addr.slice(0, 6)}…${addr.slice(-4)}`;\n}\n\nfunction timeAgo(ts: bigint) {\n  const seconds = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) - Number(ts);\n  if (seconds < 60) return 'just now';\n  const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);\n  if (minutes < 60) return `${minutes}m ago`;\n  const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);\n  if (hours < 24) return `${hours}h ago`;\n  return `${Math.floor(hours / 24)}d ago`;\n}\n\nexport function TipFeed() {\n  const { data, isLoading, isError } = useReadContract({\n    address: TIP_JAR_ADDRESS,\n    abi: tipJarAbi,\n    functionName: 'getRecentTips',\n    args: [50n],\n    query: {\n      // Poll so the feed reflects new tips (including from other accounts).\n      refetchInterval: 4000,\n    },\n  });\n\n  const tips = (data as Tip[] | undefined) ?? [];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card\">\n      <h2>Tip feed</h2>\n\n      {isLoading && <div className=\"empty\">Loading tips…</div>}\n\n      {isError && (\n        <div className=\"notice error\">\n          Couldn&apos;t read the tip jar. Is your wallet connected to the local\n          Anvil network (chain 31337) and is the contract deployed?\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {!isLoading && !isError && tips.length === 0 && (\n        <div className=\"empty\">No tips yet — be the first to drop one. 🫙</div>\n      )}\n\n      {tips.map((tip, i) => (\n        <div className=\"tip\" key={`${tip.timestamp}-${i}`}>\n          <div className=\"tip-head\">\n            <span className=\"amount\">\n              {formatUnits(tip.amount, USDC_DECIMALS)} USDC\n            </span>\n            <span className=\"addr\" title={tip.tipper}>\n              {shortAddr(tip.tipper)}\n            </span>\n          </div>\n          {tip.message && <div className=\"message\">{tip.message}</div>}\n          <div className=\"time\">{timeAgo(tip.timestamp)}</div>\n        </div>\n      ))}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2183,"cache_read_input_tokens":35968,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2183},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d5c57ae0-5ba4-42e5-9286-490e29d2bfcb","uuid":"13590a9e-724e-4b90-892d-9a433036c8bf","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:11:46.480Z","request_id":"req_011CdMa2pdpJKyCQn6Av9jLA"}

[];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card\">\n      <h2>Tip feed</h2>\n\n      {isLoading && <div className=\"empty\">Loading tips…</div>}\n\n      {isError && (\n        <div className=\"notice error\">\n          Couldn&apos;t read the tip jar. Is your wallet connected to the local\n          Anvil network (chain 31337) and is the contract deployed?\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {!isLoading && !isError && tips.length === 0 && (\n        <div className=\"empty\">No tips yet — be the first to drop one. 🫙</div>\n      )}\n\n      {tips.map((tip, i) => (\n        <div className=\"tip\" key={`${tip.timestamp}-${i}`}>\n          <div className=\"tip-head\">\n            <span className=\"amount\">\n              {formatUnits(tip.amount, USDC_DECIMALS)} USDC\n            </span>\n            <span className=\"addr\" title={tip.tipper}>\n              {shortAddr(tip.tipper)}\n            </span>\n          </div>\n          {tip.message && <div className=\"message\">{tip.message}</div>}\n          <div className=\"time\">{timeAgo(tip.timestamp)}</div>\n        </div>\n      ))}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

sVrTSwFMeP78vB154KhkmCtjFn/pmCZiBEG2ztClqpwxwWHpDE/mRpwmRSLxCATaiuibfPB3gihN0L5bF1MecV5RLmln49A0c9W1W/nB0o66jqCBgB"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1282,"cache_read_input_tokens":38151,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1282},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d5c57ae0-5ba4-42e5-9286-490e29d2bfcb","uuid":"b54de928-8bf2-4cf2-9498-b273aba71185","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:11:54.771Z","request_id":"req_011CdMa3qTwp6noShHqEMjeF"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMa3rqJMMJEBCZ1zA8at","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now the tip form with the approve → tip flow and a faucet:"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1282,"cache_read_input_tokens":38151,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1282},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d5c57ae0-5ba4-42e5-9286-490e29d2bfcb","uuid":"cd8088e8-13cd-4774-854a-ca32a3fc82ed","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:11:54.775Z","request_id":"req_011CdMa3qTwp6noShHqEMjeF"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMa3rqJMMJEBCZ1zA8at","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01PzgRMzgv7NfxqtfPvENSMd","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T210654Z-claude-no-skill-1/workspace/frontend/components/TipForm.tsx","content":"'use client';\n\nimport { useEffect, useState } from 'react';\nimport {\n  useAccount,\n  useReadContract,\n  useWaitForTransactionReceipt,\n  useWriteContract,\n} from 'wagmi';\nimport { formatUnits, parseUnits } from 'viem';\nimport { erc20Abi, tipJarAbi } from '@/lib/abi';\nimport {\n  TIP_JAR_ADDRESS,\n  USDC_ADDRESS,\n  USDC_DECIMALS,\n  USDC_IS_MOCK,\n} from '@/lib/contracts';\n\nfunction parseAmount(input: string): bigint | null {\n  if (!input.trim()) return null;\n  try {\n    const wei = parseUnits(input, USDC_DECIMALS);\n    return wei > 0n ? wei : null;\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\nexport function TipForm() {\n  const { address, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState('');\n  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');\n\n  const amountWei = parseAmount(amount);\n\n  // --- reads: balance + allowance ---\n  const { data: balance, refetch: refetchBalance } = useReadContract({\n    address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n    abi: erc20Abi,\n    functionName: 'balanceOf',\n    args: address ? [address] : undefined,\n    query: { enabled: !!address, refetchInterval: 5000 },\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance, refetch: refetchAllowance } = useReadContract({\n    address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n    abi: erc20Abi,\n    functionName: 'allowance',\n    args: address ? [address, TIP_JAR_ADDRESS] : undefined,\n    query: { enabled: !!address },\n  });\n\n  const needsApproval =\n    amountWei !== null && (allowance === undefined || (allowance as bigint) < amountWei);\n\n  // --- writes ---\n  const approve = useWriteContract();\n  const sendTip = useWriteContract();\n  const faucet = useWriteContract();\n\n  const approveReceipt = useWaitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: approve.data });\n  const tipReceipt = useWaitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: sendTip.data });\n  const faucetReceipt = useWaitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: faucet.data });\n\n  // Refetch allowance once an approval confirms.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (approveReceipt.isSuccess) refetchAllowance();\n  }, [approveReceipt.isSuccess, refetchAllowance]);\n\n  // Clear the form once a tip confirms.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (tipReceipt.isSuccess) {\n      setAmount('');\n      setMessage('');\n      refetchAllowance();\n      refetchBalance();\n    }\n  }, [tipReceipt.isSuccess, refetchAllowance, refetchBalance]);\n\n  // Refresh balance after minting from the faucet.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (faucetReceipt.isSuccess) refetchBalance();\n  }, [faucetReceipt.isSuccess, refetchBalance]);\n\n  const busy =\n    approve.isPending ||\n    approveReceipt.isLoading ||\n    sendTip.isPending ||\n    tipReceipt.isLoading;\n\n  function onApprove() {\n    if (!amountWei) return;\n    approve.writeContract({\n      address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n      abi: erc20Abi,\n      functionName: 'approve',\n      args: [TIP_JAR_ADDRESS, amountWei],\n    });\n  }\n\n  function onSendTip() {\n    if (!amountWei) return;\n    sendTip.writeContract({\n      address: TIP_JAR_ADDRESS,\n      abi: tipJarAbi,\n      functionName: 'tip',\n      args: [amountWei, message],\n    });\n  }\n\n  function onMint() {\n    if (!address) return;\n    faucet.writeContract({\n      address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n      abi: erc20Abi,\n      functionName: 'mint',\n      args: [address, parseUnits('100', USDC_DECIMALS)],\n    });\n  }\n\n  const insufficientBalance =\n    amountWei !== null && balance !== undefined && [address] : undefined,\n    query: { enabled: !!address, refetchInterval: 5000 },\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance, refetch: refetchAllowance } = useReadContract({\n    address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n    abi: erc20Abi,\n    functionName: 'allowance',\n    args: address ? [address, TIP_JAR_ADDRESS] : undefined,\n    query: { enabled: !!address },\n  });\n\n  const needsApproval =\n    amountWei !== null && (allowance === undefined || (allowance as bigint) < amountWei);\n\n  // --- writes ---\n  const approve = useWriteContract();\n  const sendTip = useWriteContract();\n  const faucet = useWriteContract();\n\n  const approveReceipt = useWaitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: approve.data });\n  const tipReceipt = useWaitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: sendTip.data });\n  const faucetReceipt = useWaitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: faucet.data });\n\n  // Refetch allowance once an approval confirms.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (approveReceipt.isSuccess) refetchAllowance();\n  }, [approveReceipt.isSuccess, refetchAllowance]);\n\n  // Clear the form once a tip confirms.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (tipReceipt.isSuccess) {\n      setAmount('');\n      setMessage('');\n      refetchAllowance();\n      refetchBalance();\n    }\n  }, [tipReceipt.isSuccess, refetchAllowance, refetchBalance]);\n\n  // Refresh balance after minting from the faucet.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (faucetReceipt.isSuccess) refetchBalance();\n  }, [faucetReceipt.isSuccess, refetchBalance]);\n\n  const busy =\n    approve.isPending ||\n    approveReceipt.isLoading ||\n    sendTip.isPending ||\n    tipReceipt.isLoading;\n\n  function onApprove() {\n    if (!amountWei) return;\n    approve.writeContract({\n      address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n      abi: erc20Abi,\n      functionName: 'approve',\n      args: [TIP_JAR_ADDRESS, amountWei],\n    });\n  }\n\n  function onSendTip() {\n    if (!amountWei) return;\n    sendTip.writeContract({\n      address: TIP_JAR_ADDRESS,\n      abi: tipJarAbi,\n      functionName: 'tip',\n      args: [amountWei, message],\n    });\n  }\n\n  function onMint() {\n    if (!address) return;\n    faucet.writeContract({\n      address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n      abi: erc20Abi,\n      functionName: 'mint',\n      args: [address, parseUnits('100', USDC_DECIMALS)],\n    });\n  }\n\n  const insufficientBalance =\n    amountWei !== null && balance !== undefined && (balance as bigint) < amountWei;\n\n  const writeError = approve.error || sendTip.error;\n\n  if (!isConnected) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"card\">\n        <h2>Send a tip</h2>\n        <p className=\"muted\">\n          Connect your wallet to send a USDC tip and see your balance.\n        </p>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card\">\n      <h2>Send a tip</h2>\n\n      <div className=\"row\" style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>\n        <span className=\"muted\">Your USDC balance</span>\n        <strong>\n          {balance !== undefined\n            ? `${formatUnits(balance as bigint, USDC_DECIMALS)} USDC`\n            : '—'}\n        </strong>\n      </div>\n\n      <label className=\"field\">\n        <span className=\"cap\">Amount (USDC)</span>\n        <input\n          inputMode=\"decimal\"\n          placeholder=\"5.00\"\n          value={amount}\n          onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n        />\n      </label>\n\n      <label className=\"field\">\n        <span className=\"cap\">Message (optional)</span>\n        <textarea\n          placeholder=\"gm ☕ thanks for the great work!\"\n          maxLength={280}\n          value={message}\n          onChange={(e) => setMessage(e.target.value)}\n        />\n      </label>\n\n      {needsApproval ? {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMaC2HJHmAYtWY6K7tjP","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_0141FvyEs7TZdFfSbPo3qaeV","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T210654Z-claude-no-skill-1/workspace/frontend/lib/wagmi.ts","content":"'use client';\n\nimport { connectorsForWallets } from '@rainbow-me/rainbowkit';\nimport {\n  injectedWallet,\n  metaMaskWallet,\n  rainbowWallet,\n} from '@rainbow-me/rainbowkit/wallets';\nimport { createConfig, http } from 'wagmi';\nimport { localAnvil } from './contracts';\n\n// A WalletConnect projectId is required by some wallets. For purely local\n// development with an injected wallet (MetaMask) any non-empty value works;\n// set NEXT_PUBLIC_WC_PROJECT_ID from https://cloud.walletconnect.com if you\n// want WalletConnect / mobile-wallet options to function.\nconst projectId = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_WC_PROJECT_ID || 'local-dev-tip-jar';\n\n// We build a curated connector list rather than using getDefaultConfig() so we\n// can skip the Coinbase/Base-account connector (it drags in heavy optional\n// deps that aren't needed for local development).\nconst connectors = connectorsForWallets(\n  [\n    {\n      groupName: 'Recommended',\n      wallets: [injectedWallet, metaMaskWallet, rainbowWallet],\n    },\n  ],\n  { appName: 'USDC Tip Jar', projectId },\n);\n\nexport const wagmiConfig = createConfig({\n  connectors,\n  chains: [localAnvil],\n  transports: {\n    [localAnvil.id]: http(),\n  },\n  ssr: true,\n});\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":903,"cache_read_input_tokens":47775,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":903},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d5c57ae0-5ba4-42e5-9286-490e29d2bfcb","uuid":"a1806d2a-0f9c-4292-8e25-b13044c333bf","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:14:02.266Z","request_id":"req_011CdMaByvR2PhNet7drWrcT"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMaXKuyXcqC6KjWTf8aP","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01JhaxwZ7SULsgxZmWMqDgX2","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T210654Z-claude-no-skill-1/workspace/Makefile","content":"# Convenience wrapper around the local dev workflow. See README.md for the\n# manual, step-by-step version and MetaMask setup.\n\n# Anvil's first prefunded account (deployer). Local test key only — never use\n# this on a real network.\nDEPLOYER_PK ?= 0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80\nRPC_URL     ?= http://127.0.0.1:8545\n\n.PHONY: install chain deploy test frontend clean\n\ninstall: ## Install contract libs and frontend deps\n\tcd contracts && forge install\n\tcd frontend && npm install\n\nchain: ## Start a local anvil chain (leave running in its own terminal)\n\tanvil\n\ntest: ## Run the contract tests\n\tcd contracts && forge test -vv\n\ndeploy: ## Deploy MockUSDC + TipJar to the running chain and write frontend config\n\tcd contracts && forge script script/Deploy.s.sol:Deploy \\\n\t\t--rpc-url $(RPC_URL) --broadcast --private-key $(DEPLOYER_PK)\n\nfrontend: ## Start the Next.js dev server on http://localhost:3000\n\tcd frontend && npm run dev\n\nclean: ## Remove build artifacts\n\tcd contracts && forge clean\n\trm -rf frontend/.next\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":348,"cache_read_input_tokens":61770,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":348},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d5c57ae0-5ba4-42e5-9286-490e29d2bfcb","uuid":"b97ad6bb-1d18-4762-a29a-c04224ec8a25","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:18:09.521Z","request_id":"req_011CdMaXJSvVa3vfHPj3mg1U"}

Local test key only — never use\n# this on a real network.\nDEPLOYER_PK ?= 0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80\nRPC_URL     ?= http://127.0.0.1:8545\n\n.PHONY: install chain deploy test frontend clean\n\ninstall: ## Install contract libs and frontend deps\n\tcd contracts && forge install\n\tcd frontend && npm install\n\nchain: ## Start a local anvil chain (leave running in its own terminal)\n\tanvil\n\ntest: ## Run the contract tests\n\tcd contracts && forge test -vv\n\ndeploy: ## Deploy MockUSDC + TipJar to the running chain and write frontend config\n\tcd contracts && forge script script/Deploy.s.sol:Deploy \\\n\t\t--rpc-url $(RPC_URL) --broadcast --private-key $(DEPLOYER_PK)\n\nfrontend: ## Start the Next.js dev server on http://localhost:3000\n\tcd frontend && npm run dev\n\nclean: ## Remove build artifacts\n\tcd contracts && forge clean\n\trm -rf frontend/.next\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMaY9UwydhddjrxPWDQo","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now the main deliverable — the README:"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":705,"cache_read_input_tokens":62118,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":705},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d5c57ae0-5ba4-42e5-9286-490e29d2bfcb","uuid":"d54dccd2-8b74-4fd9-ac7b-6d0205bd99e4","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:18:13.991Z","request_id":"req_011CdMaY5zbdNczWW71cX6xy"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMaY9UwydhddjrxPWDQo","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01WFdfgn7VsQGsDgVkt84S8t","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T210654Z-claude-no-skill-1/workspace/README.md","content":"# USDC Tip Jar (Base)\n\nA small full-stack dApp: an onchain **TipJar** contract that accepts **USDC**\ntips with a message, and a web app with a live tip feed, a send-a-tip form, and\na connect-wallet flow.\n\nOn Base mainnet the jar is meant to point at the canonical USDC token\n`0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`. This repository runs the whole\nthing **locally** against a [Foundry](https://book.getfoundry.sh/) `anvil`\nchain — nothing is deployed to a live network. Because a fresh local chain has\nno real USDC, local setup deploys a small `MockUSDC` (6 decimals) with an open\nfaucet so you can mint yourself test USDC.\n\n```\nworkspace/\n├── contracts/                 # Foundry project (Solidity)\n│   ├── src/TipJar.sol         # the tip jar\n│   ├── src/MockUSDC.sol       # local-only test USDC with a mint() faucet\n│   ├── script/Deploy.s.sol    # deploy + write frontend config\n│   └── test/TipJar.t.sol      # contract tests\n├── frontend/                  # Next.js + wagmi + RainbowKit + viem\n│   └── lib/deployedContracts.json  # addresses (written by the deploy script)\n├── Makefile                   # convenience targets\n└── README.md\n```\n\n## Prerequisites\n\n- **Foundry** (`forge`, `anvil`, `cast`) — install:\n  `curl -L https://foundry.paradigm.xyz | bash && foundryup`\n- **Node.js 20+** and **npm**\n- **MetaMask** (or another injected browser wallet) to send tips from the UI\n\n## Setup — run it locally\n\nYou'll use **three terminals**: one for the chain, one to deploy, and one for\nthe web app. Run all commands from the `workspace/` root.\n\n### 1. Start the local chain (terminal 1)\n\n```bash\nanvil\n```\n\nLeave this running. It prints 10 prefunded test accounts and their private keys.\nIt listens on `http://127.0.0.1:8545` with **chain id 31337**.\n\n### 2. Deploy the contracts (terminal 2)\n\n```bash\ncd contracts\nforge install            # first time only: pulls forge-std\nforge test               # optional: run the contract tests\nforge script script/Deploy.s.sol:Deploy \\\n  --rpc-url http://127.0.0.1:8545 \\\n  --broadcast \\\n  --private-key 0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80\n```\n\nThat private key is anvil's first prefunded account (a well-known local test\nkey — never use it on a real network). The script:\n\n1. deploys `MockUSDC`,\n2. deploys `TipJar` pointing at it, and\n3. writes the addresses to `frontend/lib/deployedContracts.json`.\n\nOn a freshly started anvil chain the addresses are deterministic, so the\nvalues checked into `deployedContracts.json` already match a clean deploy:\n\n| Contract | Address |\n| --- | --- |\n| MockUSDC | `0x5FbDB2315678afecb367f032d93F642f64180aa3` |\n| TipJar   | `0xe7f1725E7734CE288F8367e1Bb143E90bb3F0512` |\n\n### 3. Start the web app (terminal 3)\n\n```bash\ncd frontend\nnpm install\nnpm run dev\n```\n\nOpen **http://localhost:3000**. The tip feed and stats read straight from the\nchain and will render even before you connect a wallet.\n\n### 4. Connect a wallet and configure MetaMask\n\n1. **Add the local network** in MetaMask → *Add network manually*:\n   - Network name: `Anvil Local`\n   - RPC URL: `http://127.0.0.1:8545`\n   - Chain ID: `31337`\n   - Currency symbol: `ETH`\n2. **Import a test account**: MetaMask → *Import account* → paste one of the\n   private keys anvil printed (e.g. account 0's key above). Each account starts\n   with 10,000 test ETH for gas.\n3. Back in the app, click **Connect Wallet** and select MetaMask.\n\n### 5. Send a tip\n\n1. Click **Mint 100 test USDC** (the faucet button, shown only in local/mock\n   mode) to give your account some USDC.\n2. Enter an amount and an optional message.\n3. USDC uses a two-step flow, so the button first shows **Approve USDC** — click\n   it and confirm. Once the approval confirms it becomes **Send tip** — click\n   and confirm.\n4. Your tip appears at the top of the feed within a few seconds (the feed\n   polls the chain), and the totals update.\n\n> **Tip:** import a second anvil account into MetaMask to tip from a different\n> address and watch the shared feed update for both.\n\n## Contract overview (`TipJar.sol`)\n\n- `tip(uint256 amount, string message)` — pulls `amount` of USDC from the caller\n  (requires prior `approve`), records the tip, and emits `NewTip`.\n- `getRecentTips(uint256 count)` — the feed, newest first.\n- `tipCount()`, `totalTipped()`, `tippedBy(address)`, `balance()` — read helpers.\n- `withdraw()` — owner-only; sends the jar's full USDC balance to the owner.\n- `transferOwnership(address)` — owner-only.\n\nUSDC transfers are checked for both a `false` return and a revert; a failed\ntransfer leaves the tip unrecorded.\n\n## Handy Make targets\n\n```bash\nmake chain      # anvil\nmake test       # forge test\nmake deploy     # deploy + write frontend config\nmake frontend   # next dev\n```\n\n## Using real Base USDC (optional, still local)\n\nTo exercise the real USDC contract, run anvil as a **fork** of Base and tell\nthe deploy script to use the canonical address instead of the mock:\n\n```bash\n# terminal 1 — fork Base mainnet\nanvil --fork-url https://mainnet.base.org\n\n# terminal 2 — deploy against real USDC\ncd contracts\nUSDC_ADDRESS=0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913 \\\nforge script script/Deploy.s.sol:Deploy \\\n  --rpc-url http://127.0.0.1:8545 --broadcast \\\n  --private-key 0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80\n```\n\nThe deploy script skips `MockUSDC` when `USDC_ADDRESS` is set, and the app\nhides the faucet button (you'd fund yourself by impersonating a USDC holder on\nthe fork with `cast`). This project intentionally does **not** deploy to any\nlive network.\n\n## Troubleshooting\n\n- **Feed shows a read error** — make sure `anvil` is running, you've run the\n  deploy step, and MetaMask is on the `Anvil Local` (31337) network.\n- **\"Nonce too high\" / stale state in MetaMask** — you restarted anvil. In\n  MetaMask: *Settings → Advanced → Clear activity tab data* for the imported\n  account, and re-deploy (fresh anvil ⇒ same deterministic addresses).\n- **WalletConnect warning in the console** — harmless locally. Set\n  `NEXT_PUBLIC_WC_PROJECT_ID` in `frontend/.env.local` (see `.env.example`) if\n  you want the WalletConnect option to work.\n\n## Notes / limitations\n\n- `MockUSDC` is a minimal ERC-20 for local testing only and has an open `mint`.\n- The frontend targets only the local chain (31337); swap `localAnvil` in\n  `frontend/lib/contracts.ts` and redeploy to point elsewhere.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":705,"cache_read_input_tokens":62118,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":705},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d5c57ae0-5ba4-42e5-9286-490e29d2bfcb","uuid":"f15041de-01f9-457f-8234-d3c694e56c02","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:18:50.378Z","request_id":"req_011CdMaY5zbdNczWW71cX6xy"}

The script:\n\n1. deploys `MockUSDC`,\n2. deploys `TipJar` pointing at it, and\n3. writes the addresses to `frontend/lib/deployedContracts.json`.\n\nOn a freshly started anvil chain the addresses are deterministic, so the\nvalues checked into `deployedContracts.json` already match a clean deploy:\n\n| Contract | Address |\n| --- | --- |\n| MockUSDC | `0x5FbDB2315678afecb367f032d93F642f64180aa3` |\n| TipJar   | `0xe7f1725E7734CE288F8367e1Bb143E90bb3F0512` |\n\n### 3. Start the web app (terminal 3)\n\n```bash\ncd frontend\nnpm install\nnpm run dev\n```\n\nOpen **http://localhost:3000**. The tip feed and stats read straight from the\nchain and will render even before you connect a wallet.\n\n### 4. Connect a wallet and configure MetaMask\n\n1. **Add the local network** in MetaMask → *Add network manually*:\n   - Network name: `Anvil Local`\n   - RPC URL: `http://127.0.0.1:8545`\n   - Chain ID: `31337`\n   - Currency symbol: `ETH`\n2. **Import a test account**: MetaMask → *Import account* → paste one of the\n   private keys anvil printed (e.g. account 0's key above). Each account starts\n   with 10,000 test ETH for gas.\n3. Back in the app, click **Connect Wallet** and select MetaMask.\n\n### 5. Send a tip\n\n1. USDC uses a two-step flow, so the button first shows **Approve USDC** — click\n   it and confirm. Once the approval confirms it becomes **Send tip** — click\n   and confirm.\n4. Your tip appears at the top of the feed within a few seconds (the feed\n   polls the chain), and the totals update.\n\n> **Tip:** import a second anvil account into MetaMask to tip from a different\n> address and watch the shared feed update for both.\n\n## Contract overview (`TipJar.sol`)\n\n- `tip(uint256 amount, string message)` — pulls `amount` of USDC from the caller\n  (requires prior `approve`), records the tip, and emits `NewTip`.\n- `getRecentTips(uint256 count)` — the feed, newest first.\n- `tipCount()`, `totalTipped()`, `tippedBy(address)`, `balance()` — read helpers.\n- `withdraw()` — owner-only; sends the jar's full USDC balance to the owner.\n- `transferOwnership(address)` — owner-only.\n\nUSDC transfers are checked for both a `false` return and a revert; a failed\ntransfer leaves the tip unrecorded.\n\n## Handy Make targets\n\n```bash\nmake chain      # anvil\nmake test       # forge test\nmake deploy     # deploy + write frontend config\nmake frontend   # next dev\n```\n\n## Using real Base USDC (optional, still local)\n\nTo exercise the real USDC contract, run anvil as a **fork** of Base and tell\nthe deploy script to use the canonical address instead of the mock:\n\n```bash\n# terminal 1 — fork Base mainnet\nanvil --fork-url https://mainnet.base.org\n\n# terminal 2 — deploy against real USDC\ncd contracts\nUSDC_ADDRESS=0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913 \\\nforge script script/Deploy.s.sol:Deploy \\\n  --rpc-url http://127.0.0.1:8545 --broadcast \\\n  --private-key 0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80\n```\n\nThe deploy script skips `MockUSDC` when `USDC_ADDRESS` is set, and the app\nhides the faucet button (you'd fund yourself by impersonating a USDC holder on\nthe fork with `cast`). {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMacULsgbtS9SYS9eFMr","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01C3cYSndmDrxARmKE2ncSJF","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/.claude/projects/-Users-spencerfaber-dev-ethskills-evals/memory/rainbowkit-wagmi-next-x402-build-fix.md","content":"---\nname: rainbowkit-wagmi-next-x402-build-fix\ndescription: RainbowKit+wagmi+Next.js dev build fails on @x402 / async-storage optional deps — fix with IgnorePlugin\nmetadata:\n  type: reference\n---\n\nRainbowKit v2 + wagmi v2 + Next.js (webpack) dev server throws `Module not found: Can't resolve '@x402/evm/upto/client'` (and `@x402/core/client`, `@x402/evm/exact/client`). Chain: `@rainbow-me/rainbowkit` → `wagmi/connectors` barrel → `@wagmi/connectors/baseAccount` → `@base-org/account` → `@coinbase/cdp-sdk` lazily `import()`s x402 payment modules that aren't installed. The barrel import means avoiding the Coinbase/Base connector (e.g. via `connectorsForWallets`) does NOT fix it.\n\nFix in `next.config.js` webpack (resolve.alias `false` did NOT work for these subpath dynamic imports; IgnorePlugin does):\n\n```js\nwebpack: (config, { webpack }) => {\n  config.plugins.push(new webpack.IgnorePlugin({ resourceRegExp: /^@x402\\// }));\n  // also silences a harmless @metamask/sdk RN warning:\n  config.plugins.push(new webpack.IgnorePlugin({ resourceRegExp: /^@react-native-async-storage\\/async-storage$/ }));\n  return config;\n}\n```\n\nAlso push `'pino-pretty','lokijs','encoding'` to `config.externals`. The ignored modules are never executed at runtime for injected/MetaMask flows.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1379,"cache_read_input_tokens":65842,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1379},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d5c57ae0-5ba4-42e5-9286-490e29d2bfcb","uuid":"821ff4ba-2ac4-422a-94e0-6706d75f3f80","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:19:25.813Z","request_id":"req_011CdMacNV9jm2oZuGpauLfv"}

Chain: `@rainbow-me/rainbowkit` → `wagmi/connectors` barrel → `@wagmi/connectors/baseAccount` → `@base-org/account` → `@coinbase/cdp-sdk` lazily `import()`s x402 payment modules that aren't installed. The barrel import means avoiding the Coinbase/Base connector (e.g. via `connectorsForWallets`) does NOT fix it.\n\nFix in `next.config.js` webpack (resolve.alias `false` did NOT work for these subpath dynamic imports; IgnorePlugin does):\n\n```js\nwebpack: (config, { webpack }) => {\n  config.plugins.push(new webpack.IgnorePlugin({ resourceRegExp: /^@x402\\// }));\n  // also silences a harmless @metamask/sdk RN warning:\n  config.plugins.push(new webpack.IgnorePlugin({ resourceRegExp: /^@react-native-async-storage\\/async-storage$/ }));\n  return config;\n}\n```\n\nAlso push `'pino-pretty','lokijs','encoding'` to `config.externals`. The ignored modules are never executed at runtime for injected/MetaMask flows.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false,"memdirStamped":true}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMaeCEZWPrkX7hRiRALD","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Everything is built, running, and verified. Here's what I delivered.\n\n## USDC Tip Jar — done and running locally\n\n**Contracts** (`contracts/`, Foundry):\n- `TipJar.sol` — accepts USDC tips with a message, keeps an onchain feed (`getRecentTips`, newest-first), tracks totals/per-address, owner-only `withdraw`/`transferOwnership`. Transfers are checked for both `false`-return and revert. Documented to use Base USDC `0x8335…2913` in production.\n- `MockUSDC.sol` — minimal 6-decimal ERC-20 with an open `mint` faucet, for local dev only (a fresh anvil chain has no real USDC).\n- `Deploy.s.sol` — deploys both, then writes addresses to `frontend/lib/deployedContracts.json`. Honors `USDC_ADDRESS` env to skip the mock and use real USDC on a Base fork.\n- `test/TipJar.t.sol` — **10 tests, all passing** (tip flow, feed ordering, approval/balance reverts, owner withdraw/access control, ownership transfer).\n\n**Frontend** (`frontend/`, Next.js + wagmi + RainbowKit + viem):\n- Connect-wallet flow (curated injected/MetaMask/Rainbow connectors), tip feed with live polling, stats (total tipped / count), and a send form implementing USDC's two-step approve → tip flow, plus a \"Mint 100 test USDC\" faucet button in mock mode.\n\n**Verification performed:**\n- `forge test` → 10/10 pass; `tsc --noEmit` → clean.\n- anvil (chainId 31337) + deploy + full mint/approve/tip cycle over `cast` → tip recorded and readable.\n- Next dev server → **HTTP 200**, zero module warnings, UI markers present, and the exact `getRecentTips` read path returns the seeded tip.\n\n**Still running now:** anvil (`:8545`) and the web app at **http://localhost:3000**. To stop them: `pkill -f \"next dev\"` and `pkill anvil`.\n\n**README.md** documents prerequisites, the 3-terminal setup, MetaMask network + account import, the tip flow, the contract API, a `Makefile`, and an optional \"real Base USDC via fork\" path. Nothing is deployed to any live network, as required.\n\nTwo notes worth flagging:\n- One demo tip (\"gm from cast\") is in the currently-running anvil's memory; it vanishes when anvil restarts, so a fresh README run starts with an empty feed — expected.\n- The RainbowKit/wagmi stack needed a webpack `IgnorePlugin` in `next.config.js` to stub optional `@x402/*` payment deps that the transitive Coinbase connector references but never uses locally; I saved that gotcha to memory since it'll recur with this stack."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":477,"cache_read_input_tokens":68781,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":477},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d5c57ae0-5ba4-42e5-9286-490e29d2bfcb","uuid":"a097f66d-bba4-4967-9a78-084bcbd5ecb8","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:19:48.100Z","request_id":"req_011CdMaeB8putXvm1x4fBBez"}
